- [New Graduate :mortar_board:](/NEW_GRAD_USA.md) - **632** available ([FAANG+](/NEW_GRAD_USA.md#faang), [Quant](/NEW_GRAD_USA.md#quant), [Other](/NEW_GRAD_USA.md#other))
